# Windows — inferred

The **7,063** projects that probably run on Windows but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [2,968 confirmed ones](windows.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Windows](windows.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **4** of 10, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](windows-inferred.md) · [2](windows-inferred-2.md) · [3](windows-inferred-3.md) · **4** · [5](windows-inferred-5.md) · [6](windows-inferred-6.md) · [7](windows-inferred-7.md) · [8](windows-inferred-8.md) · [9](windows-inferred-9.md) · [10](windows-inferred-10.md) — [continue on page 5 →](windows-inferred-5.md)
